    "newString": "Alternative strategies (DALS, MBR, DGS) fail to beat greedy (Appendix~\\ref{app:dals}); these negative results are specific to fine-tuned models with constrained decoding (Appendix~\\ref{app:when_selection_works}).",
    "originalFile": "\\section{Discussion}\\label{sec:discussion}\n\nEffective selection likely requires content-aware verifiers \\cite{cobbe2021verifiers, venktesh2025trust}.\nThree factors constrain selection: degeneracy (17.8--67.9\\%), limited headroom, and LP-quality alignment ($\\rho$ 0.043--0.667); within non-degenerate instances, MBR utility correlates with F1 ($\\rho{=}0.524$) \\cite{eikema2022sampling}.\nWhere LP compression is mitigated (range ${\\geq}0.05$, $n{=}210$), LP yields $+2.9$~pp ($p{=}0.004$; Table~\\ref{tab:nontied_main}), though $\\varepsilon{=}0.05$ is post-hoc ($p_{\\text{adj}}{=}0.14$; Appendix~\\ref{app:epsilon_robustness}).\nTemperature disentangles degeneracy from alignment: at $T{=}1.2$, degeneracy drops to 14.9\\% yet LP does not improve ($\\rho{=}0.225$; Table~\\ref{tab:t_degeneracy}); the selection window narrows with model improvement \\cite{wang2023selfconsistency, snell2025scaling}.\n\n\\paragraph{From diagnosis to practice.}\\label{sec:selection_method}\nEntity-level construction bypasses both failure mechanisms (Table~\\ref{tab:entity_construction}). Degeneracy gates effectiveness: across seven conditions (two model families, two prompting regimes, three datasets), gains are positive when degeneracy ${<}22$\\% and absent when ${>}31$\\% (all $p{<}0.01$; epoch ablations confirm within-dataset, Appendix~\\ref{app:epoch_confound}). However, under 72B few-shot, construction fails despite low degeneracy, indicating that demonstrations alter diversity structure in ways that undermine entity-level voting; the mechanism is not simple error correlation (Appendix~\\ref{app:fs_error}). QE signals remain effective under few-shot (SJ $\\rho$ 0.29--0.44), confirming that quality estimation is regime-independent even when construction is not.\nAlternative strategies (DALS, MBR, DGS) fail to beat greedy (Appendix~\\ref{app:dals}); our negative results are specific to fine-tuned models with constrained decoding (Appendix~\\ref{app:when_selection_works}).\n",

    "originalFile": "\\section{Discussion}\\label{sec:discussion}\n\nEffective selection likely requires content-aware verifiers \\cite{cobbe2021verifiers, venktesh2025trust}.\nThree factors constrain selection: degeneracy (17.8--67.9\\%), limited headroom, and LP-quality alignment ($\\rho$ 0.043--0.667); within non-degenerate instances, MBR utility correlates with F1 ($\\rho{=}0.524$) \\cite{eikema2022sampling}.\nWhere LP compression is mitigated (range ${\\geq}0.05$, $n{=}210$), LP yields $+2.9$~pp ($p{=}0.004$; Table~\\ref{tab:nontied_main}), though $\\varepsilon{=}0.05$ is post-hoc ($p_{\\text{adj}}{=}0.14$; Appendix~\\ref{app:epsilon_robustness}).\nTemperature disentangles degeneracy from alignment: at $T{=}1.2$, degeneracy drops to 14.9\\% yet LP does not improve ($\\rho{=}0.225$; Table~\\ref{tab:t_degeneracy}); the selection window narrows with model improvement \\cite{wang2023selfconsistency, snell2025scaling}.\n\n\\paragraph{From diagnosis to practice.}\\label{sec:selection_method}\nEntity-level construction bypasses both failure mechanisms (Table~\\ref{tab:entity_construction}). _source line 6681 · 2026-05-20T10:14:18.198Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_012BLVjNUUTCcqZAHhezhJfT`</summary>

```text
30	    Five signals from three families evaluated on four NER benchmarks, two model families, and a 4B ablation, achieving reliable quality estimation ($\rho$ up to 0.45, AUROC up to 0.82).
31	    QE enables selective prediction: filtering low-confidence instances at 80\% coverage yields $+5$--$8$\,pp F1 (area under the precision-recall curve [AUPRC] 0.70--0.92, VC strongest; \S\ref{sec:prescriptive}).
32	
33	    \item \textbf{Failure modes of test-time scaling for structured prediction.}
34	    For fine-tuned 4B--8B models, LP-quality alignment (within-instance $\rho(\text{LP}, \text{F1})$) is the primary predictor of selection success: LP achieves $+1.39$~pp on Few-NERD ($p{<}0.001$) where alignment is strong, but not on benchmarks with weak alignment.
35	    Instance degeneracy causally modulates LP within fixed settings (epoch and temperature ablations), but does not predict cross-dataset direction ($\rho{=}0.052$, 23 configurations); structural signals fail by construction; supervised combinations and hidden state probes (Appendix~\ref{app:hidden_probe}) confirm the gap persists.
36	    The resulting three-step diagnostic (measure degeneracy, assess signal alignment, choose aggregation) provides a reusable protocol for structured test-time scaling (\S\ref{sec:discussion}).
37	
38	    \item \textbf{Entity-level construction as a partial mitigation for structured test-time scaling failures.}
39	    The diagnostic naturally motivates entity-level aggregation, adapting entity voting from ensemble NER \cite{florian2003named} to stochastic sampling: under fine-tuning and zero-shot regimes, LP-weighted construction yields $+1.40$\,pp on Few-NERD and $+1.02$\,pp on SciERC (multi-seed mean, $p{<}0.005$; \S\ref{sec:prescriptive}).
40	    Construction and LP selection are complementary, governed by LP-quality alignment.
41	\end{enumerate}
```
